[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

Nevertheless, inevitably one of the more aggressive students will stalk into the area, point his weapon accusingly at one of those on the wall, and the two run off, one giving chase to the other.

The area is illuminated.

[Combat Lounge]
The wooden walls of this room are covered with posters and pictures of figures in various combat positions. They exhibit a wide range of possible tactics that can be used with various weapons. A grizzled veteran, looking quite bored, leans against one of the walls and seems to be only interested in making sure the trainees fighting does not spill into the rest of the guild. A couple of couches are spread around the room to provide rest for those seeking to catch their breath, but a large sign on the northern wall indicates anyone with serious injuries should report to the healer across the central hall. An archway leads outside to the penned-in training area that are available to practice combat skills.

The area is illuminated.

[Combat Training Area, Basement]
The southwest corner of the basement is quiet, save for several slimes in the corner peacefully dissolving a large pile of wooden training swords. A disgusting stench envelopes the area, as the wood dissolves under the slimes, only slightly relieved by the vent in the ceiling.

A couple of couches are spread around the room to provide rest for those seeking to catch their breath, but a large sign on the northern wall indicates anyone with serious injuries should report to the healer across the central hall. An archway leads outside to the penned-in training area that are available to practice combat skills.

The area is illuminated.

[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

Some of the pine cones have grown to be the size of watermelons and would take two or more Fae to carry them.

The area is illuminated.

[Lightning Forest]
The large pine trees grow in abundance creating a canopy of green above. The thick foliage causes an illusion of dusk on the forest floor, even when the sun shines brightly above. As night falls, even the moon is obliterated by the pine trees and darkness turns to the deep pitch black of nightmares. Wafting through the air on and off is the acrid smell of charred bark.


The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, east

[Lightning Forest]
The stand of pines grow thicker. Those who walk through here can hear a resounding crunch as their feet step upon the very thick bed of pine needles. An occasional flower struggles to reach light after breaking through the thick needles, yet their colors remain muted as no relief is found in their struggle. On occasion a glimpse of light flashes from the south, but either direction on the barely discernible path remains dark.

The area is illuminated.

[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

The cavern floor has not been smoothed by the constant travel of those walking through, nor by the many carts dragged along the floor. The uneven rock creates an unsure footing. The area is illuminated.

[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

[Ancient Graveyard]
Paralleling the widening path are empty holes in the ground. The realization that these empty holes once held not only a coffin, but a body, becomes abundantly clear by the broken wood and scattered bone fragments still lying around. The large oak trees that surround these desecrated graves stand silently, their only purpose to drop their leaves and fill in the gaping holes.


The area is illuminated.

[Graveyard Commons]
A large hole lies in the center of the path. Surrounding the hole are rotting corpses artistically displayed in different positions. Whomever placed them here did so in in a ritualistic manner. Over-grown rats chew at the marred flesh of the corpses and then scurry off into the darkness.

Against the tree line sits a small black granite bench. An old fountain, full of black water, buzzing with mosquitoes and smelling of a city sewer, stands in the center of the area. A large mausoleum can be seen to the north.

A raised wooden platform sits off to the western side of the bonfire, topped with a banner reading, "WELCOME TO THE FESTIVAL OF LIGHTS!"

The area is illuminated.

[Urda, Southern Gatehouse]
City Guards in perfectly pressed uniforms stand on each side of the large black gate. Respectfully and with a smile they randomly search travelers as they pass through the large stone arch.  Upon completion they nod subtly exclaiming "Thank you for your cooperation. Enjoy your stay!"

The area is illuminated.

[Southern Cross Road]
Neatly kept cobblestones lead north and south the cracks between them free of dirt and debris. The large black gate which allows entrance to the city from Travers towers above several trees to the south. To the north the road curves slightly then begins to straighten. The road both north and south is lined on either side by brass lanterns which hang close to the ground from dark walnut posts allowing for plenty of light even on the darkest nights. To the west and the east a neat but narrower road appears to parallel the tall city walls which dots the sky in the distance.

The area is illuminated.

[Merchants Run]
The neatly manicured stone street bustles with activity as the sounds of market resonate excitedly. Travelers, merchants and city residents gather in a foray of discussion over their latest purchases, as the varying smells of cured leathers, hot metal, and baked spices mingle overhead in the air. A small shop constructed in black shingles attempts to blend into the shadows on the southern side of the street, its large wooden door beckoning patrons to enter. A small wooden sign posted above the door reads "Double Boil"

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: east, door, arch

[Merchants Run]
Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained road. Through the open facade young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. Above the archway a small but functional sign reads "Malinar's Magic and Mayham".

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, east, portal, open door

[Merchants Run]
An eye-catching black striped tiger is emblazoned on the facade of lacquered wooden building on the north side of the road. Its shoulders raised high, and face frozen in a fierce roar. Through the many, high-arched, steel frame, windows a wooden figure can be seen wearing the latest gentleman's fashion. Grey smoke rises from a single stone chimney.A simple plaque hanging from a twisted wrought iron rod reads, "Regal Fashion". Smooth stone steps lead to the entrance. On the south side of the street a large brick chimney funnels white billowy smoke into the air projecting the aroma of fresh baked goods into the area. Through the large bay window a burly human man can be seen kneading and forming doe and placing his creations into an oven. The heavy mahogany door is propped open seemingly to allow for relief from the ovens intense heat.

The area is illuminated.

Three large benches made of wood and stone have been placed around the fountains circumference. Down the entire length of the Central Guild hallway the walls are adorned with intricate portraits of recent Guild graduates. Upon first notice the portraits seem to be stationary, but as another eager student passes from the Guild an older image quickly morphs into the face of the new graduate. Between every portrait, gold hilted torches illuminate the eerily life-like illustrations, causing their newly polished nameplates to shine. A small wooden door on one side bears the sign: "Jeebz' Diskownt Markit."

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north, open squeaky hinged door

s

[Lounge]
Plush couches and armchairs are scattered around the room, arranged haphazardly into impromptu groupings. Around the room are small clusters of obviously flustered trainees, chatting amongst themselves about the examiners and various spell techniques. A small fireplace in the eastern wall both warms those near it and softly illuminates the hall, casting a comforting glow on everything in sight. To the left of the fireplace sits a door, a sign poorly nailed to the wall over it marking it as the trainers' housing. On the other side of the exit hangs a cork board dotted with messages written on scraps of faded paper in every language.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st city waste engineer, 2nd city waste engineer

[Trainers Office]
This singular room is a hub of learning. It is lined with an array of doors, each leading into variously sized examination. Housed inside each of the small rooms is a teacher waiting to impart their knowledge upon trainees of all shapes and sizes. There is a simple pair of signs outside of each office, the first identifying the skill to be tested and the second explaining how one may practice the skill to improve if they have previously failed to advance a level. An iron staircase leads to an upper catwalk which provides access to other training areas in the building. Torches set along the walls in between the doors provide illumination across the room.

The area is illuminated.

[Entrance Hall]
Upon first sight, it is clear the entrance hall would seem awe-inspiring to even the most regal of any realm. Made entirely out of a soft golden-yellow marble, the walls seem to swirl and shine as if made of a golden fire when hit by any source of light. Both sides of the room are decorated with idealized stone statues of the varied races of the Realm, all seeming to try to show themselves off to those who walk by. Two brilliant golden pillars flank the path into the Lounge, welcoming one to its grandeur.  To the west is the guild master's room, where all trainees venture when they feel they have graduated and are ready to leave the Hero's Guild.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: north

[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly to empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the worlds races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts, with wrought iron lanterns, look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard, coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: northwest, south, southeast, east, northeast, north, open grate, open off-center wooden door

se

[Combat Lounge]
The wooden walls of this room are covered with posters and pictures of figures in various combat positions. They exhibit a wide range of possible tactics that can be used with various weapons. A grizzled veteran, looking quite bored, leans against one of the walls and seems to be only interested in making sure the trainees fighting does not spill into the rest of the guild. A couple of couches are spread around the room to provide rest for those seeking to catch their breath, but a large sign on the northern wall indicates anyone with serious injuries should report to the healer across the central hall. An archway leads outside to the penned-in training area that are available to practice combat skills.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st city waste engineer
Obvious exits are: northwest, west, wooden ladder, massive steel portcullis, stone stairs

[North Central Hall]
The central hallway of the Heroes Guild is decorated with portraits and busts of heroes of the Old Kingdom.  The pictures fill up nearly every inch of available wall space with distinguished-looking Cyrwian men and women. Along the walls darkened bronze sconces illuminate the portraits. Each portrait bears a bronze plaque with a name etched upon it, but years of neglect have rendered them tarnished and unreadable. From the east, are the faint cries and shouts of pitched combat, and, as if on cue, another young cadet rushes in and immediately out, heading west to the healer's room.

The area is illuminated.

Two brilliant golden pillars flank the path into the Lounge, welcoming one to its grandeur.  To the west is the guild master's room, where all trainees venture when they feel they have graduated and are ready to leave the Hero's Guild.

The area is illuminated.
